De naturalium effectuum causis, sive de incantationibus (1556, Latin)
=====================================================================
Pietro Pomponazzi — De naturalium effectuum causis, sive de incantationibus (On the Causes of Natural Effects, or On Incantations), 1556 edition, Latin text, public domain. The great naturalistic critique of magic; pivotal for demonology and the philosophy of magic. Scan via Internet Archive.
